More surprisingly, 96:16 converted the act `كذّب` into the enduring description `ناصية كاذبة`, and 96:17–18 showed that `تولى` was not simple disappearance: it pivoted into council mobilization and thus activated `و ل ي:B003` rival authority. The later sequence made denial bodily attributable and withdrawal politically productive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002","root_000531/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b3bdae0c27cd85d30f95","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:13:1\",\"root_000531\",[\"B002\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Üçüncü 'gördün mü' sorusu, inkâr ile yüz çevirmeyi ilahî görüşten önce tanığa sunulan son hüküm konusu yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B001","root_001290/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bf53e26ac69a61fab9f8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:13:3\",\"root_001290\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yalanlama, doğru okumaya karşı sözle kurulmuş sahte bir anlatı üretme girişimi gibi de işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007","root_000531/B013","root_001290/B001","root_001290/B002","root_001684/B003","root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d1a489ec636510e2f406","text":"1. The third inquiry tests denial plus bodily/social turning away. Evidence: `ر ء ي B013` alerting formula; `ك ذ ب B002` declaring false/denying, `ك ذ ب B001` falsehood; `و ل ي B007` turning away/withdrawal. Structural cue: this ayah mirrors 96:11-12: if the other was on guidance/commanded taqwa, what if this one denied and turned away? Mechanism: denial is not merely a proposition; it produces directional withdrawal from guidance and worship. Abductive move: the packet supplies denial and turning branches; I infer that falsehood has a posture. Reading change: the ayah reads as a diagnosis of a whole movement away from truth. 2. `تولى` also activates authority and loyalty, preserving an alternate reading: he may turn away by assuming a rival charge. Evidence: `و ل ي B003` assuming authority, `و ل ي B004` alliance/support, `و ل ي B006` turning face/attention toward, contrasted with `و ل ي B007` withdrawal. Structural cue: the same form can imply turning oneself toward another alliance while away from guidance. Mechanism: the opponent's denial is sustained by social backing, later explicit in `ناديه`. Abductive move: the packet gives multiple `ولي` directions; I infer coexistence of retreat from guidance and alignment with a faction. Reading change: `تولى` reads both as retreat and as taking up the wrong patronage/side. 3. `كذب` activates fabricated speech against divine creation. Evidence: `ك ذ ب B001/B002` falsehood/denial; `خ ل ق B007` fabricated false speech; `ق ر ء B001/B002` gathered true recitation. Structural cue: the surah began with reading by the Creator and now shows the counter-speech of denial. Mechanism: denial fabricates a rival verbal world, attempting to unmake the authority under which reading occurs. Abductive move: the packet supplies false-speech and creation branches; I infer a speech-conflict mechanism. Reading change: the ayah becomes not only rejection but anti-recitation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f3099a72785c00f90331","text":"2. **Active invalidation rather than a merely false utterance.** `ك ذ ب:B002` supplies declaring a person or report false, denying, or disproving; this fits the intensive `كذّب`. Guidance and the command to taqwa are the immediately preceding alternatives, and 96:16 later calls the seized forelock `كاذبة`.
